# CarolinaDB — Implementation Audit (2026-09-12)

**Scope:** every specification `md/SPEC-001` … `SPEC-014`, the owner checklists (`md/FALTA.md`, `md/REVISAR-02.md`, `md/REVISAR.md`) and the status documents (`README.md`, `docs/STATUS.md`, `models/README.md`, `SECURITY.md`), audited against the working tree of this repository on 2026-09-12.

**Method.** Sixteen targets, one auditing agent each: it read the document in full and classified every normative requirement, named structure and acceptance-table row as `implemented`, `partial`, `missing` or `not_applicable`, citing file paths and test/campaign names found with `grep`/`ls`. `docs/STATUS.md` was never accepted as evidence. The rules were: `implemented` requires code **and** a test or campaign that asserts the property; code without a test is `partial`, and the row says so; `not_applicable` only where the spec itself scopes the item out of v1 or the feature is deliberately disabled. Effort: small < 1 day, medium = a few days, large = a SPEC-014 stage.

**Verification status.** Two adversarial verifiers were run per target: a *code-exists* lens that opens every cited file and checks the mechanism really does what the requirement says, and a *tests-prove-it* lens that must find the test asserting the property or downgrade the row. Both default to refuting: what they cannot confirm inside the files goes down a level, and a downgrade from either overrules the auditor. Thirty of the thirty-two runs completed (1921 rows confirmed, 155 refuted, 156 corrections applied below); SPEC-013 has no verifier pass and SPEC-012 only the code lens, so those two targets are auditor-reported and should be read as the weakest rows in the table. The maintainer session separately repaired the claims of `README.md`, `docs/STATUS.md`, `models/README.md` and the SPEC headers while updating them.

**Reading the counts.** Items are requirement-sized, not effort-sized: one `missing` C3 transfer protocol weighs as much in the table as one `implemented` error code. The percentages are the auditor coverage estimates for the normative surface, not a progress bar. `present in code != implemented capability != qualified capability` (SPEC-014 §1). An independent narrative audit of the same tree, written by the project owner on the same day, is [relatorio-completo.md](relatorio-completo.md); it reaches the same verdict from a different direction.

## Changes after this audit

The tables below describe the tree of 2026-09-12. These rows were closed on 2026-09-13 and are **not** reflected in them; everything else still stands. Evidence for each is in [STATUS.md](STATUS.md).

| Audited as | Now | What changed |
|---|---|---|
| SPEC-002 §87–§91, §105, S10 — MVCC GC and journal retention `missing` | implemented, tested | Version reclamation and segment truncation at checkpoint, with the horizon at the oldest registered snapshot and prepared work pinning the journal. |
| SPEC-002 §84/§81 — checkpoint image | defect found and fixed | The copy-on-write walk skipped dirty pages whose parent had been evicted: invisible while the journal was never truncated, data loss as soon as it was. |
| SPEC-011 §9 — catalog snapshots and log compaction `missing` | implemented, tested | A node-local image of the catalog, the applied frontier, the decisions and the retained replies; the leader drops the log prefix every voter has durably applied, and a restart resumes on that base. |
| SPEC-003 S003-A05 — footprint traps `partial` | implemented, tested | Aliasing, phantom insertion, aggregate group movement, bound-source change, reference deletion and unique absence. |
| SPEC-003 §5 — grouped aggregate bound | defect found and fixed | `GROUP BY <key> <= <bound>` could not be parsed at all: the key expression consumed the comparison. |
| SPEC-012 §11 — snapshot codec vectors `partial` | frozen | `snapshot_manifest` and `snapshot_chunk` joined the corpus. Store-level export/import is still missing and now has a stated normative blocker. |
| SPEC-010 §18 — lint negative control `partial` | implemented | `tools/spec_lint.py --self-test`, wired into CI. |
| SPEC-014 §4 — usefulness `missing` | implemented | `Q2-W1-LOCAL` fails a campaign that commits nothing. |

## SPEC-001 — Invariant-Compiled Consistency (Draft 0.2, md/SPEC-001.md)

SPEC-001 is an architectural spec with no acceptance table of its own (only the §18 family table); ids such as S003-A05, C5-021, CAT-09, QA-06 belong to other specs and are cited here only where the code itself keys a check to them. Audit basis: full read of md/SPEC-001.md, grep/ls over all 12 crates, fixtures/, models/, tools/, CI, plus a local `cargo test --workspace` run on 2026-09-12 (115 tests, 0 failed, 0 ignored; includes the real three-process C5 campaign and the 107s qualification acceptance suite). What is implemented and tested: the M0 semantic core (DSL, typed IR, interpreter, canonical artifacts, counterexample explorer), the M1 conservative compiler (closure/IDC, directed interactions, per-obligation ProofStatus, C5/C0 selection, certificate, checker, EXPLAIN), the M2 local durable slice (B+Tree/MVCC/journal kernel, CompiledBatch, RequestHome CAS, exact receipts, crash matrix) and the MVP-3 half of M6 (three-process single-IDC C5 over Raft with catalog grants). Partially present: typed identities, session-scope/observation contracts at IR level, qualification manifest/verdict/oracle machinery, FM-1/2/3 explicit-state model checkers (TLC not run). Missing: C1/C2/C3/C4 runtimes, multi-IDC publication, escrow ledger/transfer, evolution/migration, mTLS/authorization, runtime observability, benchmarks/baselines/W2–W8, fuzz harness, and all research evaluation obligations (H1–H5, E1–E5, Q7). docs/STATUS.md was used for orientation only, never as evidence.

- The tree changed under the audit: crates/carolina-storage/{kernel,journal,btree,memkernel,campaign}.rs, storage/tests/kernel.rs, carolina-compiler/src/lib.rs, carolina-cli, carolina-qualify, node/core_tests.rs, runtime/tests/local_slice.rs and fixtures/codec/* all carry 2026-09-13 mtimes (after the auditor's 2026-09-12 basis); a file crates/carolina-storage/tests/gc_debug.rs visible at the start…
- §61 CLI gap is wrong: the CLI does have tests — crates/carolina-cli/src/main.rs (compile_out_then_check_roundtrips_and_detects_tampering, plan_and_graph_commands_are_artifact_operations) and crates/carolina-cli/tests/qualification_cli.rs (4 tests). Status stays partial: the usage block (main.rs:21-34) still has no request-resolution, authority-inspection or migration-inspection command.
- Dead evidence cited in two items: ErrorCode::UnevaluableInvariant, UnenforceableInvariant (§12) and UnboundedFootprint (§82) are declared in crates/carolina-core/src/error.rs and never constructed anywhere in crates. §82 survives on UnsupportedEffect, which is asserted in carolina-lang/src/spec003_tests.rs:357/:383.
- §13/§57 stays implemented but atomic_groups is degenerate: lower.rs:1164 always emits one group holding every effect, so the golden bytes freeze only that trivial shape and no test can distinguish grouping.
- §54 evidence line 'cargo test --workspace -> 115 passed' does not match this tree: ~180 #[test] functions exist across the 12 crates. Status unchanged — Cargo.toml (12 members) and .github/workflows/ci.yml (fmt, clippy -D warnings, test on ubuntu+windows, spec_lint self-test, qualify --quick with artifact upload) carry the requirement.
- Verified-good high-risk claims: crates/carolina-node/tests/three_nodes.rs asserts only checks.len() >= 6, but campaign.rs three_process_c5 returns Err via ensure! on every violated property (C5-001/009/010/021, digest convergence), so the test is load-bearing; test code paths cited as 'core.rs tests' are legitimate (core.rs:1127 #[path = "core_tests.rs"] mod tests).

## SPEC-002 — CarolinaDB Storage Kernel (Draft 0.2), D:/DEV/CarolinaDB/md/SPEC-002.md

The native storage kernel (crates/carolina-storage) implements the durable core of SPEC-002 with real tests: versioned LE formats with CRC32C (pages, journal frames, MANIFEST.A/B), a slotted-page B+Tree with (key ASC, seq DESC) MVCC order and overflow chains, copy-on-write persistence at checkpoint, a redo-first segmented journal with torn-tail-vs-corruption discrimination, CompiledBatch/ProtocolOnlyBatch/TxnStatusRecord with digest and CAS, prepared transactions with durable decisions and IN_DOUBT recovery, 13 crash hooks, and campaigns that establish P1/P2/P3/P4/P6/P7/P8/P9 (crash matrix at every fault point, BTreeMap and MemKernel differentials) wired into carolina-qualify (Q1-P7-BTREE, Q1-CRASH-MATRIX, Q1-CORRUPTION, Q1-P8-CHECKPOINT, Q2-P9-PREPARED, Q2-P10-EPOCH, Q2-KERNEL-DIFFERENTIAL, Q2-ADMISSION). Not implemented: any concurrency/latching (S9), group commit (§55; GroupSync == Sync), MVCC GC and safety horizons (S10, §87–91), journal retention/consumers (§100–105), replication dedupe by OriginId (§103/§178), a storage transaction object with write set / read-your-writes (§36, §124, P5), ExpectedVersion CAS on user keys (§39), read-set capture (§40), diagnostic CLI commands (§129), most metrics and all tracing (§130–131), fuzz harnesses, storage formal models and benchmarks (S12). Unix file locking is absent (§78 only enforced on Windows). Milestones: S0/S2/S4/S7 done, S1/S3/S5/S6/S8/S11 partial, S9/S10/S12 missing. No file was modified; docs/STATUS.md was treated as a claim document only.

### Verifier findings

- Omitted requirement §98 (C4 CERTIFIED) — status partial: storage supplies stable snapshot (kernel.rs LocalSnapshot) and prepare/commit/abort decisions (kernel.rs prepare/commit_prepared/abort_prepared, tested by campaign.rs prepared_invisibility), but read-version capture is absent (same gap as §40; grep ReadSet/read_set/RangeToken: no matches).
- Omitted requirement §99 (C5 SERIAL) — status implemented: ordered decisions are persisted as journal decision frames (format.rs JournalRecordKind, kernel.rs commit_prepared/abort_prepared) and no consensus lives in storage (grep -i raft in crates/carolina-storage/src: none; deps are only carolina-core, carolina-wire, crc32c).
- All seven acceptance rows of md/SPEC-002.md (§173–§179) are covered by existing items; none was omitted.
- Stale gap text, status unchanged (§57, §82, P8): a direct unit test of the flush_page refusal now exists — tests/kernel.rs:343 wal_before_page_refuses_an_undurable_flush asserts the WAL-before-page error and that the flush succeeds once durable_lsn reaches page_lsn.
- Stale gap text, status unchanged (§23–§26): a buffer-pool unit test now exists (the same wal_before_page test drives BufferPool::insert_new/flush_page directly); partial still holds because pin()/unpin() (buffer.rs:211-214) are never called by the tree.
- Stale gap text, status unchanged (§80–§81, §83, S6 and S6): step 7, journal truncation under horizons, IS implemented (kernel.rs checkpoint → first_needed_segment → publish_manifest → reclaim_segments_below, with checkpoint_lsn clamped below the oldest prepared LSN). Partial still holds: no test asserts truncation, and the checkpoint is not fuzzy (no concurrent commits possible).
- Stale gap text, status unchanged (§130): mvcc_versions_reclaimed_total, oldest_snapshot_seq, journal_retained_bytes and journal_segments/bytes_reclaimed_total now exist in kernel.rs Metrics. Partial still holds: no latency/duration metrics, no checkpoint_lag_lsn, no page_checksum_failure_total, no export surface.
- New untested risk affecting P4/§177 (status left implemented): checkpoint GC is on by default (reclaim_at_checkpoint: true) and reclaims at the oldest registered snapshot, yet no test registers a snapshot across a checkpoint. Snapshot stability under GC is unproven; btree_differential exercises the tree via TreeHarness without a GC horizon.
- crates/carolina-storage/tests/gc_debug.rs contains no assertions (only println! plus unwraps) — it is a debug harness and must not be cited as evidence for any item.
- §39 (ExpectedVersion), §40 (read-set), §36/§124 (StorageTxn/begin/build_batch), §55 (group commit; GroupSync still falls through to Sync at journal.rs:288), §27/S9 (no latch type in carolina-storage), §131 (no tracing:: in the crate), §146 (no fuzz/ dir), §147 (models/ holds only FM1-FM3), §148–§152 (no benches/), §92–§93 (Namespace::Index never written), §111–§114 (DegradedStorage/ReadOnlySafety…
- Stale gap on "§57, §82, P8, S5(WAL)" (status implemented, unchanged): the claim "No direct unit test of the flush_page refusal branch" is false — crates/carolina-storage/tests/kernel.rs wal_before_page_refuses_an_undurable_flush inserts a page at lsn 10 and asserts flush_page(io,1,9) fails with a message containing "WAL-before-page", then succeeds at 10.
- Stale gap on "§69" (status partial, unchanged but for a different reason): Store::refuse_decided_txn exists (kernel.rs:727, called first thing in commit at 1190) and tests/kernel.rs duplicate_commit_of_a_decided_transaction_is_refused covers it (TxnAlreadyCommitted, seq unchanged, nothing journaled, MemKernel parity). The real remaining hole: the guard reads the durable TxnStatus record, and Comp…
- Stale gap on "§80–§81, §83, S6" and on "S6" (both partial, unchanged): "journal truncation absent; manifest.journal_segment forced to 1; segments never reclaimed" is false — see the §100/§104–§105 correction. What actually remains for S6 is that the checkpoint is not concurrent (no writers run during it, so "fuzzy" is untested) and no test asserts reclamation.
- Stale gap on "§130" (partial, unchanged): Metrics (kernel.rs:249-270) now also carries mvcc_versions_reclaimed_total, journal_segments_reclaimed_total, journal_bytes_reclaimed_total, journal_retained_bytes and oldest_snapshot_seq. Still absent: any latency/duration metric, checkpoint_lag_lsn, journal group size, page_checksum_failure_total, and any export surface.
- Stale gap on "§111–§114" (partial, unchanged): Readiness::Failed IS entered by code — Store::readiness() (kernel.rs:1095-1098) returns Failed whenever the recovery_required fence is set by a failed durable write. Opening / DegradedStorage / ReadOnlySafety remain unreachable except via set_readiness.
- Refinement on "§17–§19" (partial, unchanged): FLAG_SYSTEM is in fact set (kernel.rs install_status_record:897 and install_protocol_mutation:935/948). Only FLAG_HAS_CAUSAL_META, FLAG_HAS_SERIAL_META and FLAG_HAS_ESCROW_META (batch.rs:46-48) are declared and never set or read anywhere.
- Confirmed still-missing mechanisms (greps over crates/ returned nothing): PageLatch/latching (§27, S9), StorageTxn/build_batch/PlanContext/begin (§36, §124), ExpectedVersion on user keys (§39), read-set or range tokens (§40), OriginId ever read by the kernel (§95/§178 — batch.rs:610 journals it only), tracing::/log:: in carolina-storage (§131), group commit (journal.rs:288 still folds GroupSync i…
- Unaudited omission — §98 (C4 CERTIFIED) has no row. Storage is required to supply stable snapshot + read-version capture + prepare + commit/abort decision; snapshot/prepare/decision exist, read-version capture does not (same hole as §40). Suggested status: partial.
- Unaudited omission — §99 (C5 SERIAL) has no row. Requirement: storage persists ordered decisions and does not implement consensus. TxnStatusRecord/TxnPhase + decision_ref persistence satisfy it and carolina-storage contains no consensus code. Suggested status: implemented.
- Dead test file: crates/carolina-storage/tests/gc_debug.rs (gc_debug_scan) contains no assertions at all — only println! of before/after scan sizes and journal file names. It is not evidence for any requirement and reads as leftover debugging in the committed test suite.
- Confirmed §123/§125 gap: grep for capabilities() across all of crates/ returns zero call sites, so KernelCapabilities is declared but no runtime code or test disables a plan on an unsupported obligation.

## SPEC-003 — Invariant & Effect IR (Draft 0.2, md/SPEC-003.md)

SPEC-003 is substantially implemented in crates/carolina-lang (parser → typed lowering → canonical IR/hashes → reference interpreter → bounded counterexample explorer) with the seven §12 fixtures frozen as golden bytes and all cited tests passing (cargo test -p carolina-core -p carolina-lang -p carolina-compiler: 54 tests green). Obligation export (§11) and IDC closure (§8) live in carolina-compiler; request identity (A07) is exercised end-to-end by carolina-runtime/tests/local_slice.rs. Main gaps: no fuzz harness for the IR/DSL decoders (A09 missing); footprint tests for aliasing, phantom insertion, aggregate group movement and reference deletion are absent (A05); A11 cross-group session composition is only enforced at lowering, not as a G1/G2 compiler test; DomainSelector lacks Range/Predicate key sets and there is no exported DependencyHypergraph/Hyperedge; Monotonic invariants have no pre/post evaluator; operation-version immutability ((OperationId,version) semantic change rejection) is not checked anywhere; UnknownNormalizer/UnboundedFootprint/UnevaluableInvariant error codes are defined but never raised; several effect primitives (Assign, AddToSet, RemoveFromSet, CompareAndSwap, Delete/index maintenance, partial-release rejection) have code but no fixture test.

### Verifier findings

- Evidence the audit missed entirely: crates/carolina-lang/src/spec003_tests.rs (487 lines, wired in lib.rs as `#[cfg(test)] mod spec003_tests;`) closes gaps the audit lists as open under §3 primary key, §3.1 name resolution, §4 Option (OPTIONAL read), §6 set/CAS effects, §6 Reserve/Release partial-release rejection, §6 Transfer same-endpoint rejection, S003-A02, S003-A03 and S003-A09.
- Stale gap text (status unchanged): the '§9 session scope' gap says no compiler test hits UnsupportedSessionScope, but crates/carolina-compiler/src/lib.rs:503 composite_session_scope_is_refused_as_unsupported asserts exactly that; the '§13 resource limits' gap says no compiler test for BudgetExceeded, but lib.rs:448 budget_exhaustion_yields_unknown_never_proven asserts Unknown(BudgetExceeded) for…
- §9 session scope stays partial for a reason the audit files under A11: SPEC-003 §9 requires that all causal prerequisites and session observations fit the one resolved group; analysis.rs (SESSION arm, :723-776) only inspects the contract's own session_scope and never compares a RequiresFact prerequisite's group with the operation's group.
- §11 evidence quality: IR-DEF (analysis.rs:544) and IR-FOOT (:572) are pushed as unconditional `proven` for every operation/template with a generic premise string ('reference interpreter evaluates every IR node with checked arithmetic'), i.e. no per-operation definedness or footprint check backs those two families.
- §13 limits: SPEC-003 §13 requires an explicit limit for 'expansion nodes'; crates/carolina-core/src/limits.rs has no such field at all (only payload/depth/collection/scalar/source/declarations/expr_depth/set_cardinality/analysis_budget), and max_set_cardinality appears nowhere outside limits.rs.
- §8 atomic groups: lower.rs (~:1163) hard-codes footprint.atomic_groups to a single group containing every effect index, so the composite/atomic-group edge §8 contemplates cannot be expressed per-effect-set (consistent with atomicity: WholeInvocation being the only accepted value, lower_contract:1653).
- Confirmed still absent, as the audit says: no DependencyHypergraph/Hyperedge type anywhere in crates (grep), KeySet has only Point/All (ir.rs:522), ErrorCode::UnknownNormalizer / UnboundedFootprint / UnevaluableInvariant are never constructed outside error.rs, interp.rs never reads IndexIR (no index maintenance on Delete), and no .cdl fixture uses REFERENCE or GROUP BY.
- No omitted acceptance rows: md/SPEC-003.md lines 339-349 contain exactly S003-A01..A11 and all eleven appear in the audit items.
- §9 session scope: the stated gap 'No compiler test hits UnsupportedSessionScope' is false — crates/carolina-compiler/src/lib.rs:503 composite_session_scope_is_refused_as_unsupported asserts ErrorCode::UnsupportedSessionScope and panics if a plan is produced. Status stays partial only because an undeclared cross-group case is still undetected.
- §3.1 name resolution: the stated gap is stale — spec003_tests.rs lowering_rejects_structural_errors_with_typed_codes asserts DuplicateIdentity (duplicate RECORD and duplicate OPERATION) and MissingRecord (unresolved record). Status stays partial only for ambiguous/shadowed bindings (lower.rs:1019), which no test reaches.
- Gaps now closed without a status change: §4 Option/missing rows (OPTIONAL read tested in optional_reads_and_set_effects_execute) and §6 Reserve/Release + S003-A08 (partial-release and effect-level same-endpoint transfer tested in partial_release_and_same_endpoint_transfer_are_rejected_at_the_effect).
- §5 InvariantIR coverage is thinner than 'implemented' suggests: only 5 of 11 invariant_kind values occur in fixtures/golden (LowerBound, Unique, Conservation, StateTransition, CausalPrerequisite), and ScopeExpr::PerGroup is never produced — no GROUP BY appears in any .cdl or test.
- Re-verified as still accurate: UnknownNormalizer, UnboundedFootprint and UnevaluableInvariant appear only in crates/carolina-core/src/error.rs (never raised); max_set_cardinality appears only in crates/carolina-core/src/limits.rs (never read); KeySet has only Point and All; delete_user exists only in fixtures/dsl/unique_username.cdl and its golden, with no test evaluating it; crates/carolina-cata…
- Golden-freeze weakness for §10/S003-A01: fixtures.rs all_fixtures_lower_and_match_golden writes the golden files when UPDATE_GOLDEN is set or the file is absent, so a missing fixtures/golden directory silently re-freezes instead of failing.
- md/SPEC-003.md line 4 is a claim, not evidence: it states A09 is covered by mutation-fuzz, which overstates what decoder_and_frontend_mutation_fuzz_never_panics asserts (totality, canonicality, resource limits — not type/footprint preservation). docs/STATUS.md was not used as evidence anywhere in this verification.

## SPEC-004 — Coordination Compiler (Draft 0.2, 2026-09-09): closure, obligations, certificate, checker, EXPLAIN, CC0/CC1 gates

The CC0 core of SPEC-004 exists and is tested: typed CompileInput/TopologySnapshot, union-find closure with fixed point, per-candidate obligation judgments (11 kinds, Proven/Disproven/Unknown with 6 UnknownReasons), bounded pair exploration with replayed counterexamples, deterministic closure-level selection (C5 baseline, C0 replacement), canonical OperationPlan/ConsistencyCertificate with astra.plan.v1/astra.certificate.v1 domains, an artifact checker, EXPLAIN, and CLI compile/explain/check; the Q0-DETERMINISM campaign runs in CI. Only C0_LOCAL and C5_SERIAL are qualified; C1/C2 are analyzed but unselectable, C3 escrow synthesis (§8), C4 certification (§9), composite multi-IDC atomicity (§11.1), structured MigrationRequirements (§14), refinement relation (§3), higher-arity/exclusion/drain edges, golden EXPLAIN fixtures (A13), mixed-family simulation (A14) and `carolina plan`/`graph invariants` are missing. Several checker gaps remain (premises, evidence hashes, compatibility rules not verified). Acceptance: A01/A03/A05 implemented; A02/A04/A07/A08/A09/A10/A11/A12/A13/A14/A15/A16 partial; A06 not applicable (C4 deferred). All 10 compiler tests, 19 lang, 25 core and 9 runtime local_slice tests pass locally.

('§5 counterexample replay' left implemented.)
- '§4 phase 4' (implemented, gap none): no test asserts an AtomicWith edge or a per_key=true template — grep for AtomicWith finds only analysis.rs:382 and plan.rs; the only per_key assertions (lib.rs:132, lib.rs:293) assert false. - Evidence mis-citation (status unaffected): the §2 scoped-generations item cites tools/spec_lint.py FORBIDDEN_ALIASES as policing Rust aliases, but spec_lint scans only md/SPEC-*.md and README.md (tools/spec_lint.py:328-333). The item stands as implemented on crates/carolina-core/src/ids.rs newtypes plus test epochs_are_not_interchangeable_and_do_not_wrap (ids.rs:837).
- Audit summary line 'All 10 compiler tests' is stale: crates/carolina-compiler/src/lib.rs now contains 16 #[test] functions.

## SPEC-005 — C1/C2 Runtime: Semantic Replication, Idempotent Application and Causal Observations (md/SPEC-005.md, Draft 0.2, 2026-09-09)

SPEC-005 is essentially unimplemented and the repository says so itself: the qualification runner lists C1_COMMUTATIVE and C2_CAUSAL as "MVP-5 not started" (crates/carolina-qualify/src/runner.rs:275-276), gate Q3-PROTOCOLS is recorded NOT_RUN, checker rules Q-C06/Q-C07 are NOT_APPLICABLE, and carolina-runtime's own header disclaims replication. No SemanticCommitV1, CausalContextV1, StreamCoverage, Dot, SessionTokenV1, CausalObservationV1, semantic outbox/inbox, receive/apply state machine, causal scheduler, anti-entropy, group bootstrap, or C12-* campaign exists (grep returns zero hits; only record-kind names are registered in carolina-wire and tools/spec_lint.py). What does exist and is tested are neighbouring building blocks from SPEC-002/011/012: typed non-interchangeable epochs and OriginId/IdcBinding, the durable RequestHome CAS with RequestIdentityMismatch and identical-receipt resolution (local slice and three-process C5 campaign), OutcomeUnknown/IdentityExpired handling, wire negotiation with downgrade protection and fail-closed unknown kinds, a SnapshotManifestV1 with chunk validation (semantic cut is a Null placeholder), a storage crash matrix over 13 fault points, a partial Readiness enum, storage-epoch advance, a partial error taxonomy, and compiler-side rejection of C1/C2 candidates (bounded decrement and increment_and_get counterexamples, cross-group CompositeScope mapped to UnsupportedSessionScope but untested). Of the 19 acceptance rows only C12-015 has a passing test (compile-time), 8 are partial via C5/local analogues or formal models, and 10 are missing. Gates R0-R4 are not met.

## SPEC-006 — Escrow Runtime (C3): rights ledger, transfer protocol, recovery, §16 obligations, §17 scenarios

SPEC-006 is essentially not implemented at runtime. No escrow module exists in carolina-runtime (files: catalog.rs, engine.rs, home.rs, lib.rs, state.rs only); the compiler library marks C3_ESCROW_V1 as unqualified "MVP-6" (crates/carolina-compiler/src/library.rs:124-133, test explain_mentions_candidates_and_evidence asserts "not qualified before MVP-6"), the qualify runner lists C3_ESCROW as "MVP-6 not started" and Q-C08 as NOT_APPLICABLE, and none of the ESC-001..024 ids, the §14 escrow error codes (InsufficientLocalRights, TransferPending, FencedHolder, ...) or the readiness states (RECONCILING_AUTHORITY, READY_TO_SPEND, ...) appear anywhere in crates/, fixtures/, models/ or tools/. What does exist: (1) typed identities ResourceRef/HolderRef/ResourceGeneration/EscrowEpoch/HolderAuthorityEpoch/TransferId/ReservationId with canonical round-trip and non-interchangeability tests in carolina-core; (2) an ESCROW key namespace and record-kind registrations for holder_state/transfer_terms/transfer_decision (no structs, codecs or golden fixtures); (3) the FM-1 explicit-state model (crates/carolina-models/src/fm1.rs + models/FM1_Escrow.tla) which exhaustively checks conservation T=C+U+X (H=0), credit-implies-commit, no-refund-after-commit, no passive-replica/after-close spend and the abort tombstone under T=3/two transfers with five negative controls (tests positive_model_holds_and_controls_fail, late_prepare_control_needs_the_tombstone; run by cargo test and by `carolina qualify` as gate FM-1); TLC has not been run, the model has no durability policies, causal contexts, production, crash/loss or quarantine; (4) business-level reserve/release/consume/supply on one inventory key in the local C0/C5 profile with checked arithmetic, whole-reservation-only release, ACTIVE-state checks, RequestHome CAS binding, crash-matrix fault injection and the W1 oracle conservation check — these give partial coverage of ESC-002/009/016/018/019/023/024 at the business layer only, with no rights ledger (U/H/X), no holders, no transfers, no durability policies. Gates: E0 partial (model exists, ESC-015/020-023 unmodeled, no type fixtures), E1 partial (local reservations only), E2/E3/E4 missing.

- §14 error taxonomy: the spec §14 block lists 26 distinct codes, not 27; 10 are present in crates/carolina-core/src/error.rs and 16 are absent (item says 27/17). Near-analogs exist under different names (StaleEpoch vs StaleResourceGeneration/StaleAuthorityEpoch; MissingDependency vs DependencyUnavailable) — status partial still holds.
- tools/spec_lint.py:83-85 registers HolderStateV1, TransferTermsV1 and EscrowPlanV1 but NOT TransferDecisionV1, so one of the four §4 records is not even name-tracked by the lint registry.
- fixtures/codec/codec_manifest.astj has golden_fixture_hash null for ALL 39 entries, not only the SPEC-006 records — no golden codec fixture hash exists repo-wide, so the §4-records and Gate E0 "no fixtures" gap is systemic rather than escrow-specific.
- crates/carolina-qualify/src/runner.rs also emits authority_durability_policies: ["local grant (single node)"] beside the two fields the §5.1 item cites; still a reporting string only (no AuthorityDurabilityPolicy type anywhere — grep = 0).
- §6 reservation state machine: the ACTIVE->CONSUMED edge is not a dedicated effect — it is the generic ADVANCE effect (carolina-lang/src/interp.rs AppliedKind::AdvanceState, ErrorCode::Conflict on state mismatch) used by consume() in fixtures/dsl/inventory_reserve_release.cdl. Mechanism confirmed; the item's evidence omits it. Partial-release rejection (UnsupportedEffect) is asserted only in the W…
- models/README.md states explicitly that FM-1 and FM-3 have no implementation at all, that TLC was never run, and that Q3/Q4/Q5/Q6 stay NOT_RUN even when the models pass — consistent with every "model-only" gap in the audit; the remaining ~20 partial items rest entirely on bounded FM-1/FM-3 BFS evidence with zero runtime code.
- FM-1 bounds confirmed as claimed and additionally: quantities are u8, H is structurally absent (conservation hard-codes 0), there is no durability-policy dimension and no production transition — so §16's three-evidence-kind requirement (model + deterministic simulation + real-process campaign per policy combination) has only one kind.
- No acceptance-table row was omitted: all 24 ESC rows (md/SPEC-006.md:395-418) and all 5 gate rows (:424-428) appear in the items, and every section §1-§18 is represented.
- Omitted requirement, §18 (md/SPEC-006.md:430): performance experiments after the correctness gates SHALL report denied operations while capacity exists elsewhere, transfer/rebalance overhead, offline-holder blockage and storage retention alongside throughput/latency. Status: missing — no benches directory or metric harness exists in the workspace (find -name benches = 0 hits).

Name ownership is doc-level anyway — fixtures/codec/codec_manifest.astj lists all three SPEC-006 record kinds with golden_fixture_hash null and no fixtures/codec/holder_state*.astj exists.
- Extra evidence for §1/§2 (status unchanged): crates/carolina-compiler/src/lib.rs:580 test no_safe_plan_when_no_ordered_authority_covers_the_scope proves a topology whose only authority domain is AuthorityKind::EscrowHolder compiles to NoSafePlan, i.e. C3 escrow can never be selected.

## SPEC-007 — Certified Transactions (C4): optimistic execution, predicate validation, durable reservations (Draft 0.2, 2026-09-09)

C4 certification is not implemented. SPEC-014 schedules it as optional MVP-8 and the qualification runner declares `C4_CERTIFIED` disabled ("MVP-8 not started", crates/carolina-qualify/src/runner.rs:278) with gate Q6-CERTIFICATION NOT_RUN (runner.rs:964) and checker Q-C09 NOT_APPLICABLE (checker.rs:483). carolina-runtime contains only catalog/engine/home/state (no certifier, reservation, token or snapshot-cut code). None of the spec's named structures exist in any crate (grep for PointReadToken/RangeReadToken/CertificationRecord/CertifierTxnState/SnapshotCut/key_change_revision/predicate_change_revision/covered_buckets returns nothing; tools/spec_lint.py:87-89 only registers their schema owner). No certify_* metrics, no C4-specific error codes (10 of the §12 codes absent from carolina-core ErrorCode), and no C4-0xx acceptance id anywhere outside md/SPEC-007.md. What exists and is tested: fail-closed placeholders (unqualified C4_CERTIFIED_V1 template with milestone MVP-8, plan-codec variants CertifiedCut/Certifier/Certification never constructed, handshake refusing a C4 capability), reusable building blocks owned by other specs (typed IdcBinding/epochs, RequestKey->TxnId CAS mapping with exact receipt replay and RequestIdentityMismatch, sequential REQUIRE/ENSURE/invariant evaluation with typed rejections, local durable prepare with invisible prepared state and IN_DOUBT), and the FM-2 decision/publication model (bounded, without C4 Reserved/PreparedYes states). All 20 acceptance rows and all six milestones are unmet; the whole spec is a large SPEC-014 stage.

## SPEC-008 — Serial IDC Runtime (Draft 0.2, 2026-09-09): C5 consensus adapter, single-IDC ordered execution, prepare/decision/publication, coherent reads, C5-001..024, milestones C5-A..G

The single-IDC C5 slice (MVP-3) is real and tested: a deterministic fixed-three-voter Raft adapter (propose/read_barrier/durable log, stale-leader safety) with a seeded simulator, a node that admits each Invoke as an ordered `Admit` log entry, executes it deterministically on every voter, commits a unique replicated `Decision` before replying, fails closed on digest divergence, serves ResolveRequest only after a quorum read barrier, and a replicated RequestHome with pinned epoch; a three-process kill campaign covers C5-001/009/010/021 and is wired into the qualification gate `Q3-C5`. Everything distributed beyond one IDC is absent from the runtime: no lock hierarchy/root gates, no TxnBegin/ParticipantDescriptor/PrepareVote/DecisionCertificate records (only registry names), no Installed/Publication/PublishSeen/Completion layer, no SnapshotCut/coherent multi-IDC reads, no SPEC-009 transition operations, no SPEC-013 authentication, no §19 metrics, and roughly half of the §19 typed outcomes are missing or named differently. FM-2 (Rust explicit-state checker + TLA source) models §10/§11/§15 with negative controls, which is bounded model evidence only. Net: ~30% of the normative surface implemented; C5-C..G are missing (large, SPEC-014 MVP-4/7/evaluation).

## SPEC-009 — Plan Evolution (Draft 0.2, 2026-09-09): state machine, barrier/drain, MigrationRecord/CloseCertificate, §15 EV acceptance

SPEC-009 is essentially unimplemented as a runtime protocol, and the repository says so honestly: crates/carolina-qualify/src/runner.rs:962 records gate Q5-EVOLUTION as NOT_RUN ("SPEC-009 evolution not implemented (MVP-7)") and runner.rs:291 lists "plan evolution — MVP-7 not started". What exists is (a) the full §3 identity taxonomy in carolina-core/src/ids.rs with tests; (b) the SPEC-011 control-plane substrate the migration must ride on — catalog CAS, NoScopeOverlap scope locks, grant lifecycle STAGED→ACTIVE→CLOSING→CLOSED→RETIRED, tombstones (CAT-01/02/05/12/15/16 in carolina-catalog); (c) single-generation SPEC-012 identity/receipt preservation (RequestIdentityMismatch, ResultExpired tombstones, namespace retirement, identical receipts across crash/failover) tested in carolina-runtime/tests/local_slice.rs, carolina-node campaign C5-0xx and the qualify checker; and (d) the FM-3 explicit-state model (carolina-models/src/fm3.rs + models/FM3_Migration.tla) which checks a reduced 7-phase machine (no COMPILED/VALIDATING/READY_TO_ACTIVATE/BLOCKED) with NoOverlap/NoReopen/ResultsResolvable/ActiveNeedsEvidence/NoLateAdmission invariants and five negative controls, run by `cargo test` and `qualify --quick` in CI; TLC has never been run. Absent entirely: MigrationRecord and CloseCertificate structs (only wire-registry names and a CatalogKey::Migration variant that nothing writes), CloseAuthority/ActivateMigration messages, admission fences with admitted-request frontiers, hole-preserving origin frontiers, drain/reconcile of prepares and transfers, deterministic transform + staging + CommitmentIncompatible validation, install certificates/admission_enabled gating, retirement pins, 10 of the 12 required typed errors, all four §14 CLI commands, migration metrics, and every EV-01..EV-16 schedule (no "EV-" identifier exists in any crate). Most EV rows depend on protocol families (C1/C2/C3, multi-IDC) that are themselves MVP-4/5/6 and NOT_RUN. Overall ≈12% implemented; the remainder is a full SPEC-014 stage (MVP-7) gated behind MVP-4–6.

## SPEC-010 — Qualification (Draft 0.2, 2026-09-09)

The qualification runner (crates/carolina-qualify + carolina-models + storage/consensus/node/catalog campaign modules) implements SPEC-010 faithfully for the LOCAL profile plus the single-IDC C5 slice: immutable manifest, five-status verdicts with honest gate derivation (NOT_RUN/NOT_APPLICABLE never pass), an independent hand-written W1 oracle, history checker rules Q-C01/02/03/04/05/13/14 with budget-bounded INCONCLUSIVE, a deterministic crash runner over the real storage kernel at 13 durable boundaries (P1/P2/P3/P6/P7/P9 directly, P4/P8/P10 narrowly), a deterministic Raft simulator, a real three-process C5 kill campaign, explicit-state FM-1/2/3 checkers with negative controls, bundles, delta-debugging minimizer, CLI (qualify/simulate/replay/minimize/report) with distinct exit codes, and QA-01/03/04/05/07/08/10/11 exercised by tests; CI runs the quick campaign on Linux and Windows. What is missing is essentially everything distributed and evaluative: Q-C06–Q-C12 and 20 of 22 Q-F rows have no implementation (C1/C2/C3/C4, multi-IDC, migration, sessions), no unified SimulationEnvironment for runtime adapters, no P5 evidence, no disk-full/page-root corruption injection, no fuzz harness or compiler coverage statistics, workloads W2–W8, baselines/equivalence sheets (QA-06/QA-13), performance protocol (QA-09), E1–E5, Jepsen-style checker, liveness/pressure tests, and TLC has not been run on the TLA+ sources. Gates Q0/Q1/Q2/FM/Q3-C5 pass at quick bounds; QI is NOT_RUN (security), Q3/Q4/Q5/Q7 are NOT_RUN, Q6 is disabled by design.

## SPEC-011 — Catalog, Control Plane & Authority Registry (D:\DEV\CarolinaDB\md\SPEC-011.md, Draft 0.1, 2026-09-09)

The catalog exists as one deterministic state machine (crates/carolina-catalog/src/lib.rs, 1643 lines) applied from a fixed three-voter Raft log (carolina-consensus) inside carolina-node. What is genuinely implemented and tested: the typed identity taxonomy (§2), CAS commands with expected revisions + digests, one CatalogGeneration per successful command, idempotent AdminRequestId results / IdentityConflict, phantom-safe scope-lock overlap predicate, grant lifecycle STAGED→ACTIVE→CLOSING→CLOSED→RETIRED with closed-never-reopens, request-route CAS, tombstone reuse prohibition, genesis over an operator-pinned manifest (hash verified per voter, second genesis refused), a leader read barrier, and durable Raft storage; these are exercised by unit tests, the QI-CATALOG campaign (CAT-01/02/05/12/15/16) and the real three-process campaign. Large areas are absent: artifact registry and plan lifecycle (§6), capability qualification matrix, NodeRecord/enrollment, durability policies, closure/installation evidence and activation-as-one-CAS (§7), MigrationRecord/worker claims (model-only in FM-3), watches, PINNED_OFFLINE, catalog snapshots/restore/GC/pins (§9 — log replays from index 1, no compaction), 9 of 14 required error codes, and inspection/metrics. Admission at the node checks route+active grant at proposal time but the Admit apply path ignores the carried catalog_generation, so the CAT-03 race is not fenced. 6 of 16 acceptance rows have exercising tests; CAT-04/06/08/10 are partial (model-only or type-system-only); CAT-03/07/09/11/13/14 have no code. Roughly a third of the spec is implemented with tests; the rest is SPEC-014 MVP-3 (security/capabilities), MVP-7 (evolution) or unscheduled (§9) work.

- §5 admission check steps 1-6 (status partial is right, but its gap text is factually wrong twice): the Admit apply DOES re-check the grant against committed state at apply time (core.rs:500-521 → admission_authorized, core.rs:691-728), and grant.plans is NOT empty — core.rs:270-275 fills it from the compiled module's plans and admission requires grant.plans.contains(&plan.plan_ref()), so step 3's…
- Raw-log compaction EXISTS in the consensus adapter, contradicting the 'no compaction / log replays from index 1' evidence reused in §9 CatalogSnapshotManifest, §9 snapshot install, §4 admin id retention, §5 local fences and §2 ID reuse: crates/carolina-consensus/src/lib.rs:262-269, 341-356, 419-455 (snapshot_index/compaction_horizon/compact), storage.rs:21-36,68,251 (compact_to persisted in raft.…
- Latent safety hazard from the two facts above: the catalog (entries, results/tombstones, genesis) is rebuilt only by full replay from log index 1, and Raft::new starts from the persisted snapshot_index. If Raft::compact() were ever called on a node, committed catalog state below the base would be unrecoverable — no InstallSnapshot and no CatalogSnapshotManifest. Safe today only because carolina-n…
- Overlap-predicate fail-open (basis of the §3 ScopeLock downgrade, also relevant to §4 registered predicates): an undecodable/absent `records` set in a live ScopeLock silently permits an overlapping acquisition instead of conflicting conservatively.
- Acceptance-row count in the audit summary should read 7 of 16 rows with exercising tests, not 6, once CAT-03 is corrected.
- §4 CatalogCommand/CatalogCommit (left implemented) has two schema deviations worth recording: CatalogCommit carries `changed_keys: Vec<CatalogKey>` rather than the spec's `changed_key_revisions[]` (the revision is implied by catalog_generation), and CatalogCommand has no stored `immutable_command_hash` field — it is derived by command_hash() (lib.rs:589-601, domain astra.catalog-command.v1).
- Model-only rows: CAT-04, CAT-10 and '§7 MigrationRecord / worker claims' rest solely on crates/carolina-models/src/fm3.rs, and models/README.md states plainly that a PASS is 'not evidence about the Rust implementation' and that 'FM-1 (escrow) and FM-3 (migration) have no implementation at all'. Status: missing — the admin path (core.rs:901-960 handle_admin) returns the refusal to the caller and no replan/reread discipline, retry guard or test exists. ## SPEC-012 — Request Identity, Client Protocol, Wire Encoding & Compatibility (D:\DEV\CarolinaDB\md\SPEC-012.md, Draft 0.1, 2026-09-09)

The local/single-IDC slice of SPEC-012 is substantially built and tested: request identity (RequestKey/RequestHash with server recomputation), the durable RequestHome BindIfAbsent with the exact TxnId layout, all eight ClientReplyV1 outcomes, ResolveRequest/AbsentAtBarrier, result eviction + namespace retirement, exact byte-identical FinalReceiptV1 with astra.result/receipt digests, strict canonical encoding with reencode-and-compare, the 32-byte ASTR envelope with the header-only golden vector, Hello/HelloAck downgrade protection, and a 52-vector frozen codec corpus with 200+ negative vectors run in CI on Linux+Windows (crates/carolina-wire, carolina-runtime, carolina-node, carolina-qualify, fixtures/codec). What is missing or only partial: anything requiring SPEC-013 (authentication of resolvers, transcript binding, credential rotation), SPEC-005/009 (session tokens, SessionScopeMismatch/SessionFrontierUnavailable, home migration/drain), snapshot export/import/staging (only the manifest/chunk records and chunk validation exist), Read/ReadReply frames, the semantic 1 MiB/64 KiB argument/result/token limits (defined but never enforced), deadline_budget_ms (carried, never enforced), the full field/width/enum CodecManifest, golden vectors for snapshot/certificate/token-with-holes kinds, independent-implementation verification, and the four forward/backward compatibility-matrix rows. Wire MessageKinds 12–14 exist in code but not in the spec table. Of the 14 acceptance rows, CP-02/03/09/12 are implemented with tests, CP-08 is missing, and the rest are partial.

### Verifier findings

- Gap text wrong (status unaffected) in three items — '§11 SnapshotManifestV1', '§12 Golden corpus coverage' and 'CP-12' all claim snapshot_manifest/snapshot_chunk have no frozen golden vector and appear in kinds_without_codec. They ARE frozen: fixtures/codec/snapshot_manifest.astj and snapshot_chunk.astj exist, fixtures/codec/manifest.txt lines 53-54 carry their digests, and crates/carolina-qualif…
- Corpus size is 54, not 52: fixtures/codec/manifest.txt has 54 entries and 54 .astj files exist. The audit summary, and md/SPEC-012.md's own implementation-status line ('52-vector frozen codec corpus with 208 negatives'), are stale; the test crates/carolina-qualify/src/codec_corpus.rs codec_corpus_is_frozen only asserts frozen >= 40 and negatives >= 100, so it cannot catch the drift.
- 15 of the 40 REGISTERED_KINDS (crates/carolina-wire/src/registry.rs) still have no frozen vector: migration_record, close_certificate, semantic_commit, causal_context, session_token, holder_state, transfer_terms, transfer_decision, txn_begin, prepare_vote, decision_certificate, installed, publication_certificate, publish_seen, completion_certificate.
- Omitted requirement (no audit row): SPEC-012 §5 says namespace retirement blocks new admission but 'retained historical terminal receipts may still be resolved while authorized... without hiding retained known outcome evidence'. crates/carolina-runtime/src/engine.rs try_resolve (lines 718-726) checks retirement FIRST and returns Terminal(IdentityExpired) before consulting the binding, so a retain…
- Hard-coded single-epoch assumptions in the node: crates/carolina-node/src/core.rs:717 requires route.home_epoch == RequestHomeEpoch(1) for admission, and abandon_waiters (line 614) builds the OutcomeUnknown RouteHint with epoch 1. Any home epoch bump would refuse all admission and hand back a wrong resolver hint.
- Dead invariant APIs, only exercised by unit tests, no production caller: BindingState::can_advance_to (crates/carolina-wire/src/records.rs:743, used only in crates/carolina-wire/src/lib.rs:191-193 — the engine writes the terminal binding without consulting it, relying on CAS revisions alone) and ClientReplyV1::retry_same_identity (records.rs:984, used only in lib.rs:340). Same for registry::verif…

## SPEC-013 — Security, Authentication & Trust Model (D:\DEV\CarolinaDB\md\SPEC-013.md, Draft 0.1, 2026-09-09)

SPEC-013 is essentially unimplemented and the code says so honestly: Cargo.lock contains no TLS, COSE, Ed25519, X25519 or age crate (only sha2/crc32c); crates/carolina-node/src/lib.rs:14-16 declares "SPEC-013 mTLS is not implemented"; the qualification runner records QI-SECURITY as NOT_RUN (crates/carolina-qualify/src/runner.rs:916-919) and SECURITY.md:3-6 disclaims any security qualification. No CredentialRecord, SecurityPolicy body, PermissionGrant, AuthorityEvidence, SignedPayloadV1, audit record, backup/restore, key rotation, revocation or redaction code exists, and ErrorCode (crates/carolina-core/src/error.rs) has no authorization error at all. The transport is plaintext TCP with a self-declared endpoint role (transport.rs:199-207); CatalogCommand carries a principal that is never checked (catalog lib.rs:840-935). What does exist and is tested are the identity/replay foundations SPEC-013 builds on: request_hash identity conflict (C5-010, CP-02), FinalReceipt byte immutability (CP-06/CP-12), AdminRequestId CAS idempotence (CAT-02), retired-grant/tombstone non-resurrection (CAT-16, Q-C13), Hello/HelloAck downgrade protection including foreign-cluster refusal and DEV_LOCAL-cannot-satisfy-production ordering (CP-10), and bounded canonical/frame parsers with negative vectors. None of SEC-01..16 is fully met; SEC-04, SEC-06, SEC-07, SEC-12 and SEC-13 are partial, the rest missing. Overall roughly 15% by weight; the remaining work is a full SPEC-014 security stage (TLS+identity, authorization+policy, signed tokens, audit, encrypted backup, rotation/revocation campaigns).

## SPEC-014 — Implementation Profile & Vertical Slice (md/SPEC-014.md, Draft 0.1, 2026-09-09; status line added 2026-09-12)

MVP-0, MVP-1 and MVP-2 have code plus tests and are gated by the qualify runner (Q0/Q1/Q2 derived in crates/carolina-qualify/src/runner.rs and asserted PASS by tests/acceptance.rs). All eight MVP-2 §4 schedules are covered by named tests in crates/carolina-runtime/tests/local_slice.rs plus seeded schedules in qualify/src/local.rs. MVP-3 has a real three-voter Raft, catalog state machine, ASTR/TCP node and a real three-process kill campaign (Q3-C5 gate = QI-CATALOG + Q3-CONSENSUS-SIM + Q3-C5-PROCESS + FM-2), but its exit criterion "applicable QI" is not met because SPEC-013 mTLS/authorization is entirely absent (DEV_LOCAL only; QI-SECURITY hard-coded NOT_RUN). MVP-4..MVP-8 have only bounded FM models and unqualified compiler templates; their §4 schedules, Q4-Q7 and E1-E5 research comparison (§6) do not exist (runner hard-codes NOT_RUN; baseline_configs empty). §5 codec corpus is frozen for the 23 enabled kinds (52 vectors, 208 negatives) but snapshot/restore is wire-validation only and no catalog-level plan activation binds capabilities or QI evidence. §7 claim discipline (manifest, bundles, minimizer, NOT_RUN-never-PASS, no invented numbers) is implemented and tested. Documentation is mostly honest but stale in places: README still says no campaign/FM gate has executed, STATUS test counts are outdated, A05/A09 are marked pending though tests exist, and the newly added SPEC-014 status line links to a docs/AUDIT.md that does not exist.

## CHECKLISTS — md/FALTA.md, md/REVISAR-02.md, md/REVISAR.md

The documentation-closure half of the checklists is essentially done and lint-enforced: SPEC-009 v0.2 uses the SPEC-011 taxonomy, FinalReceiptV1 has one owner, FM-1/2/3 are defined in SPEC-010 §16 and executed as bounded Rust checkers, SPEC-011–014 and SPEC-OWNERSHIP exist, tools/spec_lint.py passes in this tree and runs in CI. The code half is real for MVP-0…MVP-3: parser/IR/interpreter/compiler with golden fixtures, B+Tree/MVCC/journal/checkpoint kernel with a 13-fault-point crash matrix, local RequestHome/BindIfAbsent/receipt/resolve path with SPEC-014 §4 schedules, a 52-vector codec corpus, a three-voter Raft + catalog + real three-process single-IDC C5 campaign. Concrete gaps: no TLS/mTLS/COSE/Ed25519 code at all (SEC campaign NOT_RUN); 17 registered record kinds have no codec/vector; no store-level snapshot export/import or GC; no multi-IDC/C1/C2/C3/evolution/C4 code; several SPEC-011 §5 cases (PINNED_OFFLINE, compaction/watches, policy/capability revocation, restore) have no code or test; E1–E5 and the PostgreSQL adapter are absent; no fuzz job, SBOM, reproducible build or ARCHITECTURE-FREEZE-0.1.md; residual "AstraDB" naming in research/ and PROPOSTA (outside lint scope) and stale crate names (carolina-qual/carolina-server) in SPEC-OWNERSHIP; README contains a stale sentence contradicting its own status table.

## DOCS-CLAIMS — README.md, docs/STATUS.md, models/README.md, SECURITY.md

The bulk of the claims hold: every ✅ deliverable row in docs/STATUS.md maps to real code and, with few exceptions, to named tests; the ⬜ rows (snapshots export, GC, compaction, mTLS, MVP-4..8, SBOM) are truthfully absent; the qualify runner claims exactly Q0/Q1/Q2/FM/Q3-C5 and the acceptance test `quick_campaign_passes_q0_q1_q2_and_reports_the_rest_not_run` exercises that in-process. Stale/false sentences: (1) README lines 42-43 "No distributed capability exists yet, no SPEC-010 qualification campaign has run, and no formal model gate (FM-1/2/3) has executed" contradicts the same section and the code (single-IDC C5 exists, runner runs the campaign and FM-1/2/3 checkers); (2) models/README "the Rust implementation of those protocols — which does not exist yet" and "Gates Q3–Q6 therefore stay NOT_RUN" ignore MVP-3 and the claimed Q3-C5 gate, and no refinement mapping from core.rs Admit/Decision to FM-2 is recorded although FM-2 is a required check of Q3-C5; (3) the "Test evidence" paragraph counts are stale (files added 2026-09-12: lang 31 not 19, compiler 15 not 10, storage 20 not 17, runtime 11, qualify 12, node 11 not 1, cli 5 omitted); (4) "Latest carolina qualify --quick" numbers (14 W1 schedules, seed 1, 16 ops) match the acceptance test's reduced config, not the current --quick config (seeds 1,2; 24 ops; nth 2) and no bundle is in the tree; (5) S003-A09 is no longer pending (`decoder_and_frontend_mutation_fuzz_never_panics`) and A05 is partly covered; (6) "52 crashing cases" is the scheduled count (13×4), the campaign only requires ≥2/3 to actually crash; (7) the compiler row lists four error codes as "✅ tests" but only UnsatisfiableDurability and UnsupportedSessionScope are asserted; (8) CLI list omits `plan`/`graph invariants`; `verify`/`workload` have no test; (9) spec_lint "negative control verified" has no artifact in the tree.

## Ranked gaps by SPEC-014 stage

Every `missing` or `partial` item, grouped by the stage that owns it. Within a group, `large` items come first. Items marked *closable now* are small, unblocked and confined to existing crates (code + tests); everything else waits for its stage, an owner decision or an external dependency.
